STRATEGIC PLANNING</h1><h3>Course Checklist &amp; Action Guide</h3><p>Now that your nonprofit is legally established, use this checklist to register for funding portals, build your credibility profiles, and master the grant application cycle.</p><h3>\n<span data-name=\"pushpin\" data-type=\"emoji\">📌</span> Step 1: Verify Good Standing</h3><ul><li>\n<p>Before applying for funds, ensure your state status is active and compliant.</p>\n<ul><li><p><span data-name=\"mag\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔍</span> <strong>Check State Corporation Condition:</strong> → Verify your status with your Secretary of State to ensure you are in \"Good Standing.\"</p></li></ul>\n</li></ul><h3>\n<span data-name=\"classical_building\" data-type=\"emoji\">🏛</span> Step 2: Federal &amp; Agency Registrations</h3><ul><li>\n<p>You cannot receive federal funds without these specific registrations.</p>\n<ul>\n<li><p><span data-name=\"laptop\" data-type=\"emoji\">💻</span> <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer nofollow\" href=\"http://SAM.gov\"><strong>SAM.gov</strong></a><strong> (System for Award Management)</strong> → <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://sam.gov\">Visit </a><a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer nofollow\" href=\"http://SAM.gov\">SAM.gov</a> (Essential for federal grants &amp; getting your Unique Entity ID)</p></li>\n<li><p><span data-name=\"laptop\" data-type=\"emoji\">💻</span> <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer nofollow\" href=\"http://Grants.gov\"><strong>Grants.gov</strong></a> → <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.grants.gov\">Visit </a><a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer nofollow\" href=\"http://Grants.gov\">Grants.gov</a> (The primary database for federal opportunities) <span data-name=\"laptop\" data-type=\"emoji\">💻</span></p></li>\n<li><p><strong>Dun &amp; Bradstreet</strong> → <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.dnb.com/duns-number.html\">Lookup or Request DUNS</a><span data-name=\"registered\" data-type=\"emoji\">®</span><a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.dnb.com/duns-number.html\"> Number</a></p></li>\n<li><p><span data-name=\"laptop\" data-type=\"emoji\">💻</span> <strong>eRA Commons</strong> → <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://public.era.nih.gov/commons/\">Visit eRA Commons</a> (Required specifically for Health and Human Service Grants)</p></li>\n</ul>\n</li></ul><h3>\n<span data-name=\"sports_medal\" data-type=\"emoji\">🏅</span> Step 3: Build Credibility &amp; Credentials</h3><ul><li>\n<p>Funders check these databases to verify your legitimacy and transparency.</p>\n<ul>\n<li><p><span data-name=\"scroll\" data-type=\"emoji\">📜</span> <strong>State Solicitation License</strong> → Check with your State Charity Official (Required in many states to legally ask for donations).</p></li>\n<li><p><span data-name=\"glowing_star\" data-type=\"emoji\">🌟</span> <strong>GuideStar / Candid Listing</strong> → <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://guidestar.org\">Claim your profile on GuideStar</a> (Upload your determination letter and mission).</p></li>\n<li><p><span data-name=\"glowing_star\" data-type=\"emoji\">🌟</span> <strong>Charity Navigator</strong> → <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.charitynavigator.org\">Visit Charity Navigator</a> (Get Your Rating, and monitor that rating and transparency score).</p></li>\n<li><p><span data-name=\"glowing_star\" data-type=\"emoji\">🌟</span> <strong>Policy Commons</strong> → <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://policycommons.net/\">Visit Policy Commons</a> (For research and policy-focused organizations).</p></li>\n</ul>\n</li></ul><h3>\n<span data-name=\"homes\" data-type=\"emoji\">🏘</span> Step 4: Access Local Funding</h3><ul><li>\n<p>Local money is often easier to get than federal money.</p>\n<ul><li><p><span data-name=\"round_pushpin\" data-type=\"emoji\">📍</span> <strong>Community &amp; Economic Development / CDBG</strong> → Locate your County Portal for \"Community Block Grants.\" → Contact your local Office of Community Development.</p></li></ul>\n</li></ul><h3>\n<span data-name=\"detective\" data-type=\"emoji\">🕵</span> Step 5: Competitive Intelligence (FOIA Requests)</h3><ul><li>\n<p>Don't guess how to win; copy what works. Use the Freedom of Information Act (FOIA) or State Public Records Acts to see who won previously.</p>\n<ul><li><p><span data-name=\"page_facing_up\" data-type=\"emoji\">📄</span> <strong>Submit a Request for Proposals (RFP) Inquiry</strong> Ask for the records of the most recent completed program year. <strong>What to request (based on the CPRA template):</strong></p></li></ul>\n</li></ul><ol>\n<li><p><strong>The Final List:</strong> Funded projects, award amounts, and descriptions.</p></li>\n<li><p><strong>Board Items:</strong> Staff reports and approving votes.</p></li>\n<li><p><strong>The Scoring Rubric:</strong> See exactly how the winners were graded.</p></li>\n<li><p><strong>Award Letters:</strong> Official notifications sent to funded entities.</p></li>\n<li><p><strong>The \"Winner's\" Application:</strong> Ask for a copy of the winning grant proposal to use as a model.</p></li>\n</ol><h3>\n<span data-name=\"memo\" data-type=\"emoji\">📝</span> Step 6: Master the Scoring Rubric</h3><p>Every grant is a game for points. Structure your proposal to hit the maximum marks. <strong>Typical Point Distribution (Reference):</strong></p><ul>\n<li><p><strong>Project Description:</strong> ~30 Points (Design and Partners)</p></li>\n<li><p><strong>Results &amp; Sustainability:</strong> ~30 Points (Outputs, Outcomes, Long-term benefits)</p></li>\n<li><p><strong>Statement of Need:</strong> ~18 Points (Community description, Workforce needs)</p></li>\n<li><p><strong>Budget &amp; Narrative:</strong> ~12 Points (Alignment of costs with the project)</p></li>\n<li><p><strong>Capacity:</strong> ~10 Points (Financial documentation and Resumes)</p></li>\n</ul><h3>\n<span data-name=\"brain\" data-type=\"emoji\">🧠</span> Step 7: Tailor Your Narrative (Funder Psychology)</h3><p>Different funders want different things. Customize your \"Ask\" based on who they are. <span data-name=\"classical_building\" data-type=\"emoji\">🏛</span> <strong>Local Governments:</strong></p><ul>\n<li><p><em>Concern:</em> Duplication of services.</p></li>\n<li><p><em>Want:</em> To save money and make a big local impact.</p></li>\n</ul><p><span data-name=\"classical_building\" data-type=\"emoji\">🏛</span> <strong>State Governments:</strong></p><ul>\n<li><p><em>Concern:</em> Tax savings, health, and justice.</p></li>\n<li><p><em>Want:</em> To see partnerships.</p></li>\n</ul><p><span data-name=\"classical_building\" data-type=\"emoji\">🏛</span> <strong>Federal Governments:</strong></p><ul>\n<li><p><em>Concern:</em> Documentation and systems.</p></li>\n<li><p><em>Want:</em> Structure, data capture, and reproducibility.</p></li>\n</ul><p><span data-name=\"office\" data-type=\"emoji\">🏢</span> <strong>Corporations:</strong></p><ul>\n<li><p><em>Concern:</em> Their brand story.</p></li>\n<li><p><em>Want:</em> To know how the grant changes individual lives (faces and feelings).</p></li>\n</ul><h3>\n<span data-name=\"calendar_spiral\" data-type=\"emoji\">🗓</span> Step 8: The Grant Calendar</h3><p>Grants move in cycles. Use this schedule to prepare ahead of time.</p><ul>\n<li><p><span data-name=\"snowflake\" data-type=\"emoji\">❄</span> <strong>Beginning of Year (Jan/Feb)</strong> → <strong>Source:</strong> Corporate Grants → <em>Action:</em> Contact project coordinators immediately. [Banks, Credit Unions]</p></li>\n<li><p><span data-name=\"cherry_blossom\" data-type=\"emoji\">🌸</span> <strong>Springtime (and year-round)</strong> → <strong>Source:</strong> Foundations → <em>Action:</em> Get on their newsletter lists; introduce yourself to program officers.</p></li>\n<li><p><span data-name=\"sun_with_face\" data-type=\"emoji\">🌞</span> <strong>May - July</strong> → <strong>Source:</strong> Federal Grants → <em>Action:</em> Search <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer nofollow\" href=\"http://Grants.gov\">Grants.gov</a> keywords describing your work. Look at last year's release dates to predict this year's. <em>{</em><strong><u>JUNE (sp.):</u> Source</strong><em>: Tourism Authority, School Districts, Law Enforcement Fleet Management, Federal, State, &amp; Local Auctions </em>→<em> </em>Action: <em>GET AHEAD OF THE AUCTION CYCLE}</em></p></li>\n<li><p><span data-name=\"apple\" data-type=\"emoji\">🍎</span><strong> September </strong>→ <strong>Source:</strong> Closing and Opening Federal Grants. Banks Second Round of Giving. → <em>Action:</em> Search <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer nofollow\" href=\"http://Grants.gov\">Grants.gov</a> keywords describing your work. Contact project coordinators immediately. [Banks, Credit Unions]</p></li>\n<li><p><span data-name=\"fallen_leaf\" data-type=\"emoji\">🍂</span> <strong>October - November</strong> → <strong>Source:</strong> State Grants → <em>Action:</em> Search state websites and ask City/County offices about state pass-through opportunities.</p></li>\n<li><p><span data-name=\"snowflake\" data-type=\"emoji\">❄</span> <strong>Late Fall / Early Winter</strong> → <strong>Source:</strong> Local Grants → <em>Action:</em> Contact the Office of Community Development.</p></li>\n</ul><h3>\n<span data-name=\"check_mark\" data-type=\"emoji\">✔</span> FINAL CHECK</h3><p>Before applying for your first grant:</p><ul>\n<li><p>Are you registered in <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer nofollow\" href=\"http://SAM.gov\">SAM.gov</a> and <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer nofollow\" href=\"http://Grants.gov\">Grants.gov</a>?</p></li>\n<li><p>Have you claimed your GuideStar/Candid profile?</p></li>\n<li><p>Have you identified your County’s CDBG portal?</p></li>\n<li><p>Have you filed a FOIA request to see a winning application from last year?</p></li>\n<li><p>Is your calendar marked for the upcoming Local, State, Federal, Corporate Grant, &amp; Funding","body":"<h1>\n<span data-name=\"check_mark_button\" data-type=\"emoji\">✅</span><strong> STRATEGIC PARTNERSHIPS &amp; ADVANCED FUNDING</strong>\n</h1><h3><strong>Course Checklist &amp; Action Guide</strong></h3><p>This phase moves beyond basic registration into building the relationships, data, and assets required to secure sustainable funding.</p><h3>\n<span data-name=\"handshake\" data-type=\"emoji\">🤝</span><strong> Step 1: Establish Partnerships</strong>\n</h3><p>Funding often follows collaboration. You need a strategy for who you work with.</p><ul>\n<li><p><strong>Action:</strong> Define your partnership goals. Are you looking for program partners (to do the work with) or funding partners (to pay for the work)?</p></li>\n<li><p><strong>Action:</strong> Create a standard \"Partnership Agreement\" template.</p></li>\n</ul><h3>\n<span data-name=\"hollow_red_circle\" data-type=\"emoji\">⭕</span><strong> Step 2: Map Your Stakeholders (The Circle Chart)</strong>\n</h3><p>Identify everyone in your orbit using Megan’s Circle Chart method.</p><ul>\n<li><p><strong>Clients:</strong> Who do you serve?</p></li>\n<li><p><strong>Peers:</strong> Who works in your space?</p></li>\n<li><p><strong>Businesses:</strong> Who has the funding?</p></li>\n<li><p><strong>Serve:</strong> Where can you volunteer to build goodwill?</p></li>\n<li><p><strong>Connect/Network:</strong> Who are the connectors?</p></li>\n</ul><h3>\n<span data-name=\"glowing_star\" data-type=\"emoji\">🌟</span><strong> Step 3: Update GuideStar (Candid)</strong>\n</h3><p>If you created this in the previous phase, now is the time to optimize it for the \"Platinum\" seal.</p><ul><li><p><strong>Action:</strong> Ensure your profile includes recent financial data and demographic info.</p></li></ul><h3>\n<span data-name=\"glowing_star\" data-type=\"emoji\">🌟</span><strong> Step 4: Monitor Charity Navigator</strong>\n</h3><p>Donors use this to vet you.</p><ul><li><p><strong>Action:</strong> Check your \"Encompass Rating\" (for smaller nonprofits) or your Star Rating. Ensure your \"Impact &amp; Results\" section is filled out.</p></li></ul><h3>\n<span data-name=\"glowing_star\" data-type=\"emoji\">🌟</span><strong> Step 5: Register with Policy Commons</strong>\n</h3><ul><li><p><strong>Action:</strong> If your nonprofit deals with research, policy, or advocacy, ensure you are listed here to be visible to academic and government funders.</p></li></ul><h3>\n<span data-name=\"bar_chart\" data-type=\"emoji\">📊</span><strong> Step 6: Gather Data (Online Resources)</strong>\n</h3><p>You must prove the \"Need\" exists. Use these specific resources to build your case:</p><ul>\n<li><p><strong>Philanthropic Resources:</strong><a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer nofollow\" href=\"https://www.carnegie.org/grants/grant-inquiries/philanthropic-resources/\"> Carnegie Corporation of New York</a></p></li>\n<li><p><strong>Social Determinants of Health:</strong><a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer nofollow\" href=\"https://odphp.health.gov/healthypeople/priority-areas/social-determinants-health\"> Healthy People 2030</a></p></li>\n<li><p><strong>State Health Disparities:</strong> Search your State’s Dept of Public Health (ex.,<a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer nofollow\" href=\"https://www.cdph.ca.gov/Programs/OHE/Pages/Disparities_Landing_Page.aspx\"> California Office of Health Equity</a>).</p></li>\n<li><p><strong>Economic Disparity:</strong> Use the<a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer nofollow\" href=\"https://data.worldbank.org/indicator/SI.POV.GINI\"> Gini Index (World Bank)</a> to show wealth inequality.</p></li>\n<li><p><strong>Reference:</strong><a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer nofollow\" href=\"https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/books/NBK425844/\"> The State of Health Disparities (NCBI)</a></p></li>\n</ul><h3>\n<span data-name=\"moneybag\" data-type=\"emoji\">💰</span><strong> Step 7: Budgeting &amp; Financials</strong>\n</h3><ul>\n<li><p><strong>Action:</strong> Create a Master Operating Budget (overhead/admin).</p></li>\n<li><p><strong>Action:</strong> Create Program-Specific Budgets (restricted funds).</p></li>\n</ul><h3>\n<span data-name=\"scroll\" data-type=\"emoji\">📜</span><strong> Step 8: Governing Documents</strong>\n</h3><ul>\n<li><p><strong>Bylaws:</strong> Ensure they are up to date and signed.</p></li>\n<li><p><strong>MOUs:</strong> Draft Memorandums of Understanding for the partnerships identified in Step #1.</p></li>\n</ul><h3>\n<span data-name=\"graduation_cap\" data-type=\"emoji\">🎓</span><strong> Step 9: Grant Lists &amp; Lessons</strong>\n</h3><p>Understand the landscape before applying.</p><ul>\n<li><p><strong>Nonprofit vs. For-Profit Grants:</strong> Know the difference (Tax-exempt mission vs. Economic development).</p></li>\n<li><p><strong>Where to find Nonprofit Grants:</strong> GrantStation, <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer nofollow\" href=\"http://Grants.gov\">Grants.gov</a>, Foundation Directory Online.</p></li>\n<li>\n<p><strong>Sponsorship vs. Grant:</strong></p>\n<ul>\n<li><p>Sponsorship: Marketing dollars. They want logo placement and audience eyeballs.</p></li>\n<li><p>Grant: Philanthropic dollars. They want social impact and outcomes.</p></li>\n</ul>\n</li>\n<li>\n<p><strong>Funding Scope:</strong></p>\n<ul>\n<li><p>Sponsorships fund: Events, t-shirts, galas, media… your imagination is the limit.</p></li>\n<li><p>Grants fund: Salaries, rent, direct program costs, supplies.</p></li>\n</ul>\n</li>\n</ul><h3>\n<span data-name=\"gift\" data-type=\"emoji\">🎁</span><strong> Step 10: In-Kind Giving Strategy</strong>\n</h3><ul>\n<li><p><strong>Action:</strong> Identify what you need that isn't cash (computers, office space, legal advice).</p></li>\n<li><p><strong>Action:</strong> Create a standard receipt for in-kind donations.</p></li>\n</ul><h3>\n<span data-name=\"calendar_spiral\" data-type=\"emoji\">🗓</span><strong> Step 11: The 2026 Grant Calendar</strong>\n</h3><ul>\n<li><p><strong>Action:</strong> Create a 12-month timeline.</p></li>\n<li><p><strong>Action:</strong> Plot the release dates for Local (Winter), State (Fall), Federal (Summer), and Corporate (Jan) opportunities. (There is no hard rule for this, but this is a good way to start creating your Grant Calendar.)</p></li>\n</ul><h3>\n<span data-name=\"page_facing_up\" data-type=\"emoji\">📄</span><strong> Step 12: Create a Prospectus</strong>\n</h3><p>Stop asking for \"money\" and start selling \"opportunities.\"</p><ul>\n<li><p><span style=\"color:var(--tt-color-text-default);\"><strong>What it is: </strong>An outline of the who, what, where, when, and why of what your nonprofit is doing, for each program you're running. A prospectus answers this one question, \"Is doing this worth consideration?\"</span></p></li>\n<li>\n<p><span style=\"color:var(--tt-color-text-default);\"><strong>Why you need one:</strong></span><span style=\"color:var(--tt-color-text-red);\"> </span>A grant prospectus serves as a professional 'first date' document that allows a funder to quickly determine if your mission aligns with their goals before anyone invests time in a full proposal.</p>\n<ul><li>\n<p><strong>How It Differs From Related Documents</strong><br></p>\n<ul>\n<li><p><strong>Proposal</strong>: Asks for something specific.</p></li>\n<li><p><strong>Business plan</strong>: Operational and internal.</p></li>\n<li><p><strong>One-pager</strong>: High-level snapshot.</p></li>\n<li><p><strong>Prospectus</strong>: Strategic, comprehensive, and decision-ready.</p></li>\n</ul>\n</li></ul>\n</li>\n<li><p><span style=\"color:var(--tt-color-text-default);\"><strong>Action:</strong> Identify the Who, What, Where, When, and Why of ALL your Nonprofit's programs.</span></p></li>\n</ul><h3>\n<span data-name=\"mega\" data-type=\"emoji\">📣</span><strong> Step 13: Media Outreach Strategies</strong>\n</h3><ul>\n<li><p><strong>Action:</strong> Build a press list of local journalists who cover your topic.</p></li>\n<li><p><strong>Action:</strong> Draft a template press release for when you win your first grant.</p></li>\n</ul><h3>\n<span data-name=\"check_mark\" data-type=\"emoji\">✔</span><strong> FINAL CHECK</strong>\n</h3><ul>\n<li><p>Do you have your stakeholder circle filled out?</p></li>\n<li><p>Are your data sources bookmarked for your next proposal?</p></li>\n<li><p>Do you understand the difference between what a sponsor funds vs. a grant?</p></li>\n<li><p>Is your 2026 Calendar dir=\"auto\">There’s real money out there—and it’s not just for the big players. Every year, hundreds of grants are awarded to small businesses just like yours: from solo founders to creative entrepreneurs, from women-led startups to local food makers.</p><p dir=\"auto\">This list includes a powerful mix of <strong>national grant competitions, fellowships, and pitch opportunities</strong>. Some have application deadlines, others are open year-round. All are worth exploring if you’re looking to fund your business idea.</p><hr dir=\"auto\"><h3 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"trophy\" data-type=\"emoji\">🏆</span> Featured Grant Opportunities</h3><p dir=\"auto\">These programs are nationally recognized and open to a wide range of industries.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span> <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.uschamber.com/co/co-100\">CO—100: America’s Top 100 Small Businesses</a>\n</h4><p dir=\"auto\">Run by the U.S. Chamber of Commerce, this prestigious award honors the most inspiring and innovative small businesses in the country.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span> <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://helloalice.com/\">Hello Alice</a>\n</h4><p dir=\"auto\">One of the most popular platforms for small business grants and resources. They offer <strong>regular grant rounds</strong>, especially for underrepresented entrepreneurs.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span> <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.startpivotgrow.com/microgrant\">Start. Pivot. Grow. Microgrant</a>\n</h4><p dir=\"auto\">A funding opportunity focused on helping new or pivoting small businesses. The application is simple and the grants are ideal for startup expenses.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span> <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://freedfellowship.com/grant\">Freed Fellowship Grant</a> <em>(Small fee required)</em>\n</h4><p dir=\"auto\">This program offers grant funding and a support fellowship for emerging entrepreneurs. Note: there is a small application fee to apply.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span> <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.prosperitynow.org/programs\">Prosperity Now – Programs</a>\n</h4><p dir=\"auto\">Explore a wide range of programs supporting economic resilience and business ownership. Excellent resource for entrepreneurs from underserved communities.</p><hr dir=\"auto\"><h3 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"books\" data-type=\"emoji\">📚</span> Grant Directories: Find Even More</h3><p dir=\"auto\">If you're ready to dive deeper into available funding, this is your go-to list for <strong>massive grant directories</strong> updated regularly.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span> <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://fundsnetservices.com/grants\">2025 Grants Directory | FundsNet Services</a>\n</h4><p dir=\"auto\">A huge and well-organized collection of grants across business types, industries, and states. <strong>Use this site to search by category, keyword, or niche.</strong></p><blockquote dir=\"auto\" class=\"tiptap-blockquote-standard\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"speech_balloon\" data-type=\"emoji\">💬</span> <em>“Find grants for every type of business, state, and category. If it’s missing, they’ll add it!”</em></p></blockquote><hr dir=\"auto\"><h3 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"green_salad\" data-type=\"emoji\">🥗</span> Industry Highlight: Food, Beverage &amp; Consumer Goods</h3><p dir=\"auto\">Are you working on a packaged food brand, beverage product, or something in the consumer goods space? These grants are for you:</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span> <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.enthusefoundation.org/pitch-competition\">Enthuse Foundation Pitch Competition</a>\n</h4><p dir=\"auto\">Open to women entrepreneurs in the <strong>food, beverage, and consumer packaged goods (CPG)</strong> industries. Great for founders ready to pitch.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span> <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.stacyssnacks.com/riseproject\">Stacy’s</a><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"registered\" data-type=\"emoji\">®</span><a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.stacyssnacks.com/riseproject\"> Rise Project</a>\n</h4><p dir=\"auto\">Created to support <strong>female founders</strong> in the food and beverage industry. Offers funding and mentorship with top experts in the field.</p><hr dir=\"auto\"><h3 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"mega\" data-type=\"emoji\">📣</span> Your Next Move</h3><ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\">Pick <strong>two programs</strong> from the list above to explore in detail.</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\">Set a calendar reminder for any <strong>upcoming deadlines</strong>.</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\">Bookmark the <strong>2025 Grant Directory</strong> so you can check back regularly for new opportunities.</p></li>\n</ul><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"check_mark\" data-type=\"emoji\">✔</span> In the next lesson, we’ll look at <strong>foundations and nonprofit grant programs</strong>—another source of funding that many small businesses overlook.</p><p dir=\"auto\">Let’s keep that momentum going.</p><p dir=\"auto\"><br dir=\"auto\"></p><p dir=\"auto\"> </p>","url":"https://lesko-help-2.mn.co/posts/business-nonprofits-career-general-business-grants-funding-opportunities","postType":"coursework","publishedAt":null,"commentCount":0,"cheerCount":0,"creator":{"id":"Z2lkOi8vbWlnaHR5L1VzZXIvMTUxMjU4NDE","resourceId":"15125841","name":"Giulia May | Community Manager","avatarUrl":"https://media1-production-mightynetworks.imgix.net/asset/b227c7d1-697c-4519-b7e8-0b0f1c283b9b/58842506_880478525637716_1227268994818375680_n.jpeg?ixlib=rails-4.3.1&fm=jpg&q=100&auto=format&w=128&h=128&fit=crop&crop=faces&impolicy=Avatar"},"space":{"id":"Z2lkOi8vbWlnaHR5L0ZsZXhTcGFjZS8xODA4Mzk1OA","resourceId":"18083958","title":"Business - Nonprofits & Career"}}},{"cursor":"Mg","node":{"__typename":"Post","resourceId":"79122515","id":"Z2lkOi8vbWlnaHR5L1Bvc3QvNzkxMjI1MTU","title":"Contact These 10 Key Organizations","body":"<p dir=\"auto\">It’s time for the next steps after your <strong>free business mentor appointment</strong>, and you understand your business goals and potential funding sources.</p><p dir=\"auto\">This lesson focuses on <strong>contacting organizations</strong> that offer grants and financial assistance to help you start or grow your business. These programs provide <strong>funding, training, and resources</strong> for entrepreneurs.</p><h3 dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><mark data-color=\"rgb(54, 85, 179)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(54, 85, 179);color:inherit;\"> 1. Watch Matthews's video first! </mark></span></h3><span class=\"mighty-video-wrapper\" contenteditable=\"false\" draggable=\"false\"><a class=\"mighty-video video-overlay nohover\" href=\"https://www.youtube.com/embed/4kJSIygdy0s\" target=\"_blank\" type=\"video/youtube\" data-video-id=\"1739623289\" data-image-asset-id=\"119236311\"><img src=\"https://media1-production-mightynetworks.imgix.net/asset/d943a043-d2b1-4502-8a1c-b0823e83b46f/Screenshot_2025-02-15_at_13.41.14.png?ixlib=rails-4.2.0&amp;fm=jpg&amp;q=75&amp;auto=format\" alt=\"Video\" class=\"fr-fil fr-dib attachment fr-draggable\" data-asset-id=\"119236311\"></a></span><p dir=\"auto\">These programs are part of the <strong>\"giving economy\"</strong>—a network of <strong>government offices and nonprofits</strong> distributing funds <strong>for free</strong>. Unlike <strong>for-profit websites</strong> you might find on Google, these organizations exist to <strong>help you</strong>, not to charge you for services.</p><h3 dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><mark data-color=\"rgb(54, 85, 179)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(54, 85, 179);color:inherit;\"> 2. Contact These 10 Key Organizations </mark></span></h3><ol dir=\"auto\">\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\"> </mark></strong></span><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\">ECONOMIC DEVELOPMENT OFFICES </mark></strong></span><strong> </strong>Visit <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"https://www.eda.gov/resources/find-grant-resources?q=/resources\">eda.gov/resources</a> to find your state’s <strong>economic development office</strong>. These offices distribute grants, loans, and training programs to help businesses grow. Call for funding opportunities in your state.</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\"> APEX ACCELERATORS </mark></strong></span><strong> </strong>Visit <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.apexaccelerators.us\">apexaccelerators.us</a> to connect with <strong>APEX Accelerators</strong>, a government-funded program that helps businesses secure <strong>government contracts</strong>—a long-term funding source for your business.</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\"> SSBCI </mark></strong></span><strong>(State Small Business Credit Initiative) </strong>Visit <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"https://home.treasury.gov/policy-issues/small-business-programs/state-small-business-credit-initiative-ssbci\">home.treasury.gov</a> to locate your state’s <strong>SSBCI program</strong>, which provides <strong>grants, loans, and direct investments</strong> in small businesses. </p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\"> CAREERONESTOP</mark></strong></span><span><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\"> </mark></strong></span><strong> (DEPARTMENT OF LABOR) </strong>Visit <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.careeronestop.org\">careeronestop.org</a> to access <strong>training grants and funding</strong> for entrepreneurs. This program can help train employees or provide funding for you to learn new skills. Please Visit the <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"https://www.careeronestop.org/BusinessCenter/default.aspx\">CareerOneStop Business Center</a>.</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\"> CDFIs </mark></strong></span><strong> (Community Development Financial Institutions) </strong>Use the <strong>CDFI locator</strong> at <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.cdfifund.gov\">cdfifund.gov</a> to find government-backed banks that provide <strong>grants and low-interest loans</strong> to small businesses.</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\"> OPPORTUNITY NETWORK </mark></strong></span><strong> (MATCHED SAVINGS GRANTS) </strong>Some organizations match your savings—if you save <strong>$1, they add $5 or more</strong> to fund your business. Visit <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.cdfifund.gov\">cdfifund.gov</a> to find programs in your area.</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\"> VETERAN-OWNED BUSINESS ASSISTANCE </mark></strong></span><strong> </strong>If you’re a <strong>veteran</strong>, there are <strong>special grants and funding programs</strong> to support your business. Visit <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"https://www.sba.gov/business-guide/grow-your-business/veteran-owned-businesses\">sba.gov</a> to find <strong>Veteran Business Outreach Centers</strong> near you.</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\"> MINORITY &amp; WOMEN-OWNED BUSINESS SUPPORT </mark></strong></span><strong> </strong>There are dedicated <strong>grants for women and minority entrepreneurs</strong> available through state and nonprofit programs. Visit <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.grants.gov\">grants.gov</a> to check eligibility.</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\"> MEETUP.COM FOR ENTREPRENEURS </mark></strong></span><strong> </strong>Networking is essential. Use <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.meetup.com\">meetup.com</a> to <strong>find local entrepreneur groups</strong> and connect with mentors, investors, and fellow business owners. </p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\"> LOCAL SMALL BUSINESS DEVELOPMENT CENTERS </mark></strong></span><strong> (SBDCs) </strong>Visit <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://americassbdc.org\">americassbdc.org</a> to find <strong>Small Business Development Centers (SBDCs)</strong> that provide <strong>free business coaching, funding advice, and resources</strong> for entrepreneurs.</p></li>\n</ol><h3 dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><mark data-color=\"rgb(54, 85, 179)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(54, 85, 179);color:inherit;\"> TIP: How to Approach These Organizations </mark></span></h3><ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"telephone_receiver\" data-type=\"emoji\">📞</span> <strong>CALL FIRST:</strong> Most grants <strong>require direct contact</strong> before applying. Call and explain your business idea.</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"muscle\" data-type=\"emoji\">💪</span> <strong>BE PERSISTENT:</strong> Some programs might not be available in your area. Keep asking until you find the right fit.</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"bullseye\" data-type=\"emoji\">🎯</span> <strong>AVOID GOOGLE SCAMS:</strong> Many for-profit companies charge fees for grant applications—<strong>stick to nonprofit and government-backed resources.</strong></p></li>\n</ul><h1 dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(249, 57, These include <strong>free business centers, mentorship, targeted grants, retail partnerships</strong>, and national accelerators—many of which are not widely known.</p><p dir=\"auto\">This lesson pulls together the best options for support, funding, and certification to help you thrive.</p><hr dir=\"auto\"><h3 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"office\" data-type=\"emoji\">🏢</span> Business Centers &amp; Support Services</h3><p dir=\"auto\">These organizations provide <strong>direct, hands-on help</strong>—from business planning and funding prep to certification support and federal contract guidance.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span> <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.mbda.gov/business-resources/business-centers\">MBDA Business Centers</a>\n</h4><p dir=\"auto\">Connect with a local <strong>Minority Business Development Agency (MBDA)</strong> center for strategic support, grant navigation, and growth opportunities.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span> <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.mbda.gov/business-resources\">MBDA Business Resources</a>\n</h4><p dir=\"auto\">Full directory of tools, partners, and federal programs for minority-owned businesses.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span> <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.sba.gov/business-guide/grow-your-business/minority-owned-businesses\">SBA: Minority-Owned Business Guide</a>\n</h4><p dir=\"auto\">Explore how the <strong>Small Business Administration</strong> supports minority business owners through funding, certifications, and development programs.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span> <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://tcmet.org/\">Center for Micro-Entrepreneurial Training</a>\n</h4><p dir=\"auto\">A practical, hands-on support center for micro-businesses and early-stage entrepreneurs from underserved communities.</p><hr dir=\"auto\"><h3 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"money_with_wings\" data-type=\"emoji\">💸</span> Grants &amp; Funding for Minority Entrepreneurs</h3><p dir=\"auto\">These opportunities are tailored specifically to <strong>Black, Latino, Indigenous, and other underrepresented business owners</strong>. Some offer funding, while others provide training and visibility.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span> <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.mbda.gov/financial-resources/capital-readiness-program\">Capital Readiness Program – MBDA</a>\n</h4><p dir=\"auto\">Supports entrepreneurs in preparing for investment and funding with business development training and capital access.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span> <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://naacp.org/find-resources/grants/powershift-entrepreneur-grant\">NAACP Powershift Entrepreneur Grant</a>\n</h4><p dir=\"auto\">Direct grant support for Black entrepreneurs with scalable business ideas.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span> <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://sell.amazon.com/programs/black-business-accelerator\">Amazon Black Business Accelerator</a>\n</h4><p dir=\"auto\">Exclusive access to funding, training, and marketing support for <strong>Black-owned brands selling on Amazon</strong>.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span> <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://rebuildtheblock.org/\">Rebuild The Block</a>\n</h4><p dir=\"auto\">Emergency grants and community funding for Black entrepreneurs.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span> <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://galaxyofstars.org/galaxy-grants/\">Galaxy Grants</a>\n</h4><p dir=\"auto\">Small business funding with a focus on <strong>women and minority founders</strong>.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span> <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://operationhope.org/1-million-black-businesses/\">Operation Hope – 1 Million Black Businesses</a>\n</h4><p dir=\"auto\">A national initiative to launch and grow Black-owned businesses, offering coaching, training, and support.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span> <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://goldhouse.org/gold-house-ventures/\">Gold House Ventures</a>\n</h4><p dir=\"auto\">Resources and funding for <strong>Asian and Pacific Islander founders</strong> through venture capital and mentorship.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span> <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" distribute through retail partnerships.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span> <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://pathwaytoopportunity.com/\">Pathway to Opportunity</a>\n</h4><p dir=\"auto\">Grant and mentorship platform focused on Black, Latino, and underserved entrepreneurs.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span> <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://blackambitionprize.com/\">Black Ambition</a>\n</h4><p <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.transformgrant.org/the-grant/\">The Grant – TRANSFORM</a>\n</h4><p dir=\"auto\">Funds projects that <strong>center justice, racial equity, and systemic change</strong> in underserved communities.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span> <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" investment.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span> <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" locally.</p><hr dir=\"auto\"><h3 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"receipt\" data-type=\"emoji\">🧾</span> Certification &amp; Business Identity</h3><p dir=\"auto\">Certification can unlock <strong>government contracts, corporate supplier lists, and special funding programs</strong>.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span> <a innovative products. Explore these options based on your field:</p><h4 dir=\"auto\"><strong>Cosmetics &amp; Retail</strong></h4><ul dir=\"auto\"><li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><em>MUSE Accelerator</em> (hosted by Ulta Beauty) – Learn more</p></li></ul><h4 dir=\"auto\"><strong>Manufacturing, Export &amp; Procurement</strong></h4><ul dir=\"auto\"><li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.mbda.gov/business-resources/specialty-centers\">MBDA Specialty Centers</a></p></li></ul><h4 dir=\"auto\"><strong>Restaurants &amp; Food Businesses</strong></h4><ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://sietefoods.com/pages/sietejuntosfund\">Siete Juntos Fund</a></p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.leeinitiative.org/\">The LEE Initiative</a></p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.aapistrong.com/aapistrong-restaurant-fund\">AAPISTRONG Restaurant Fund – National ACE</a></p></li>\n</ul><hr dir=\"auto\"><h3 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"mega\" data-type=\"emoji\">📣</span> Your Next Move</h3><ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\">Identify <strong>3 opportunities</strong> that align with your identity, industry, or mission.</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\">Start a dedicated <strong>\"Grant Tracker\"</strong> to keep tabs on deadlines and requirements.</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\">Consider <strong>certifying your business</strong> as minority-owned to unlock new funding channels.</p></li>\n</ul><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"check_mark\" data-type=\"emoji\">✔</span> In the next lesson, we’ll explore tailored resources for <strong>farmers, ranchers, and rural entrepreneurs</strong>—because big dreams grow in small towns, too.</p><p dir=\"auto\">You are building more than a business. for minority, women-owned, and disadvantaged businesses.</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://netl.doe.gov/small-business\"><strong>Small dir=\"auto\">It's time to act after your HUD counselor appointment, and you understand your finances and housing goals. </p><p dir=\"auto\">This lesson focuses on <strong>contacting organizations for housing grants and financial assistance</strong>. These programs help secure funds for down payments, closing costs, and reduced-price homes. </p><h3 dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><mark data-color=\"rgb(54, 85, 179)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(54, 85, 179);color:inherit;\"> 1. Watch Matthews's video first! </mark></span></h3><h3 dir=\"auto\"></h3><span class=\"mighty-video-wrapper\" contenteditable=\"false\" draggable=\"false\"><a class=\"mighty-video video-overlay nohover\" href=\"https://www.youtube.com/embed/UilB5r5TXTE\" target=\"_blank\" type=\"video/youtube\" data-video-id=\"1737465405\" data-image-asset-id=\"157137171\"><img src=\"https://media1-production-mightynetworks.imgix.net/asset/36c24d67-ac83-483b-9ac3-5536bbf40ba8/maxresdefault.jpg?ixlib=rails-4.2.0&amp;fm=jpg&amp;q=75&amp;auto=format\" alt=\"Video\" class=\"fr-fil fr-dib attachment fr-draggable\" data-asset-id=\"157137171\"></a></span><p dir=\"auto\"><span>These programs are part of the <strong>\"giving economy\"</strong>—a network of government offices and nonprofits distributing funds at no cost. Unlike for-profit sites on Google, these organizations aim to help, not profit from your search.</span></p><h3 dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><mark data-color=\"rgb(54, 85, 179)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(54, 85, 179);color:inherit;\"> 2. Contact These 10 Key Organizations </mark></span></h3><ol dir=\"auto\">\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\"> </mark><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\">NEIGHBORWORKS</mark></strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\"> </mark></span> Visit <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.neighborworks.org\"><strong>neighborworks.org</strong></a> to find a local branch. NeighborWorks aids homebuyers with local grants, education, and resources.</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\"> STATE HOUSING FINANCE AGENCY </mark></strong></span><strong> (NC.org): </strong><span><mark data-color=\"transparent\" style=\"background-color:transparent;color:inherit;\">Visit </mark></span><a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer nofollow\" href=\"https://www.ncsha.org\"><strong>ncsha.org</strong></a><span><mark data-color=\"transparent\" style=\"background-color:transparent;color:inherit;\"> to locate your state housing finance agency, which provides grants and programs. Call for assistance with your homeownership goals.</mark></span></p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\"> FEDERAL HOME LOAN BANKS </mark></strong></span><strong> </strong><span><mark data-color=\"transparent\" style=\"background-color:transparent;color:inherit;\">Visit </mark></span><a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer nofollow\" href=\"https://www.fhlbanks.com\"><strong>fhlbanks.com</strong></a><span><mark data-color=\"transparent\" style=\"background-color:transparent;color:inherit;\"> to find your Federal Home Loan Bank, which offers grants and funding for housing, including down payment assistance. Contact them to explore options.</mark></span></p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\"> HABITAT FOR HUMANITY </mark></strong></span><strong> </strong>Check <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.habitat.org\"><strong>habitat.org</strong></a> for local Habitat for Humanity offices. This program helps you purchase a home at a reduced price in exchange for volunteer work, known as \"sweat equity.\"</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\"> USDA RURAL DEVELOPMENT (RD) </mark></strong></span><strong> </strong><span><mark data-color=\"transparent\" style=\"background-color:transparent;color:inherit;\">Visit </mark></span><a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"https://www.rd.usda.gov/\"><span><strong><mark data-color=\"transparent\" style=\"background-color:transparent;color:inherit;\">rd.usda.gov</mark></strong></span></a><span><mark data-color=\"transparent\" style=\"background-color:transparent;color:inherit;\"> for single-family housing programs in rural areas. Contact your local USDA office for information on grants and loans.</mark></span></p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\"> COMMUNITY DEVELOPMENT OFFICES </mark></strong></span><span><strong> </strong><mark data-color=\"transparent\" style=\"background-color:transparent;color:inherit;\">Contact your </mark></span><strong>county’s Community Development Office</strong><span><mark data-color=\"transparent\" style=\"background-color:transparent;color:inherit;\"> for grants to buy or renovate homes using federal funds. Call 211 or visit </mark></span><a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"https://www.211.org/\"><span><strong><mark data-color=\"transparent\" style=\"background-color:transparent;color:inherit;\">211.org</mark></strong></span></a><span><mark data-color=\"transparent\" style=\"background-color:transparent;color:inherit;\"> for your local office.</mark></span></p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\"> COMMUNITY DEVELOPMENT FINANCIAL INSTITUTIONS (CDFIs) </mark></strong></span><strong> </strong><span><mark data-color=\"transparent\" style=\"background-color:transparent;color:inherit;\">Use the CDFI locator at </mark></span><a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer nofollow\" href=\"https://www.cdfifund.gov\"><strong>cdfifund.gov</strong></a><span><mark data-color=\"transparent\" style=\"background-color:transparent;color:inherit;\"> to find local banks offering grants for low-income homebuyers.</mark></span></p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\"> FINDHELP.ORG </mark></strong></span><strong> </strong><span><mark data-color=\"transparent\" style=\"background-color:transparent;color:inherit;\">Visit </mark></span><a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"https://www.findhelp.org\"><strong>findhelp.org</strong></a><span><mark data-color=\"transparent\" style=\"background-color:transparent;color:inherit;\"> to search for local housing programs. The platform connects you with nonprofits offering free resources and grants.</mark></span></p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\"> HOUSES FOR 1$ PROGRAM </mark></strong></span><strong> </strong><span><mark data-color=\"transparent\" style=\"background-color:transparent;color:inherit;\">Look for government initiatives offering foreclosed homes for $1, along with renovation grants. Contact your local HUD office for details. </mark></span><a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"https://www.hudhomestore.gov/\"><span><strong><mark data-color=\"transparent\" style=\"background-color:transparent;color:inherit;\">https://www.hudhomestore.gov/</mark></strong></span></a></p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\"> LOCAL HOUSING AUTHORITIES </mark></strong></span><strong> </strong>Contact your local housing authority; they often manage grant programs or partner with nonprofits for housing assistance. Here is the <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"https://www.hud.gov/program_offices/public_indian_housing/pha/contacts\"><strong>hud.gov</strong></a> link.</p></li>\n</ol><h3 dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><mark data-color=\"rgb(54, 85, 179)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(54, 85, 179);color:inherit;\"> TIP: How to Approach These Organizations </mark></span></h3><ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"telephone_receiver\" data-type=\"emoji\">📞</span><strong> CALL FIRST:</strong> Most of these organizations require direct communication. dir=\"auto\">Starting a business can feel overwhelming, especially when finding funding, legal guidance, and the proper support. The key? Don’t start by asking for money—begin by asking for <strong>help.</strong> This lesson will guide you through the best <strong>free</strong> resources to get expert advice on launching your business correctly.</p><h3 dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><mark data-color=\"rgb(54, 85, 179)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(54, 85, 179);color:inherit;\"> 1. Watch Matthews's video </mark></span></h3><p dir=\"auto\"><br dir=\"auto\"></p><span class=\"mighty-video-wrapper\" contenteditable=\"false\" draggable=\"false\"><a class=\"mighty-video video-overlay nohover\" href=\"https://www.youtube.com/embed/Unc9T7DFlio\" target=\"_blank\" type=\"video/youtube\" data-video-id=\"1739623129\" data-image-asset-id=\"119236235\"><img src=\"https://media1-production-mightynetworks.imgix.net/asset/638fa426-2d17-4d60-96ca-32dac87011b6/Screenshot_2025-02-15_at_13.38.30.png?ixlib=rails-4.2.0&amp;fm=jpg&amp;q=75&amp;auto=format\" alt=\"Video\" class=\"fr-fil fr-dib attachment fr-draggable\" data-asset-id=\"119236235\"></a></span><h3 dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><mark data-color=\"rgb(54, 85, 179)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(54, 85, 179);color:inherit;\"> 2. Make an Appointment With </mark><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(54, 85, 179)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(54, 85, 179);color:inherit;\">a Free Business    Mentor</mark></strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(54, 85, 179)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(54, 85, 179);color:inherit;\"> </mark></span></h3><p dir=\"auto\">The first step to success is <strong>talking to the right experts.</strong> <span><strong>Nonprofit organizations</strong> across the country offer</span> free business mentoring, legal assistance, and funding guidance. Here’s how to get started:</p><ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"mag_right\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔎</span> <strong>SEARCH</strong> | Visit <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"https://americassbdc.org/\">AmericasSBDC.org</a>, <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"https://www.score.org/\">Score.org</a>, and/or <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"https://www.sba.gov/local-assistance\">SBA.gov/Local-Assistance</a> (Under Construction) and enter your ZIP code to find <strong>nonprofit business advisors</strong> near you.</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"memo\" data-type=\"emoji\">📝</span> <strong>LOCATE</strong> | Choose a business mentorship location and check their website for contact details and appointment options.</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"e-mail\" data-type=\"emoji\">📧</span> <strong>CHECK EMAIL</strong> | After reaching out, you’ll receive an email from your chosen mentor with details for your in-person, phone, or Zoom consultation.</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"file_folder\" data-type=\"emoji\">📁</span> <strong>GATHER INFORMATION</strong> | Organize your business idea, potential funding needs, and any key questions you have before your appointment.</p></li>\n</ul><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"bulb\" data-type=\"emoji\">💡</span> <strong>TIP:</strong> Create folders to store all your business-related documents in one place.</p><h3 dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><mark data-color=\"rgb(54, 85, 179)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(54, 85, 179);color:inherit;\"> 3. Seek Advice from </mark><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(54, 85, 179)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(54, 85, 179);color:inherit;\">Multiple Experts</mark></strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(54, 85, 179)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(54, 85, 179);color:inherit;\"> </mark></span></h3><h4 dir=\"auto\">\n<span><mark data-color=\"transparent\" style=\"background-color:transparent;color:inherit;\">Not all mentors will have the same insights or solutions. If one doesn’t provide the information you need, don’t stop—seek out another. There are different </mark></span><strong>specialized resources</strong><span><mark data-color=\"transparent\" style=\"background-color:transparent;color:inherit;\"> for various business types and backgrounds:</mark></span>\n</h4><ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"check_mark_button\" data-type=\"emoji\">✅</span> <strong>Veterans</strong> | If you’re a veteran, check out the <strong>Veterans Business Outreach Center (VBOC)</strong> for additional business counseling and resources. <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"https://www.sba.gov/local-assistance/resource-partners/veterans-business-outreach-center-vboc-program#id-find-your-local-veterans-business-outreach-center\">Veterans Business Outreach Center (VBOC)</a></p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"check_mark_button\" data-type=\"emoji\">✅</span> <strong>Individuals with Disabilities</strong> | Special nonprofit organizations offer tailored business support. <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"https://www.disabilitysmallbusiness.org/\">National Disability Institute</a></p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"check_mark_button\" data-type=\"emoji\">✅</span> <strong>Rural Businesses &amp; Farmers</strong> | Visit <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"https://www.farmers.gov/your-business/beginning-farmers\">Farmers.gov</a> to find grants and programs for small businesses in rural areas.</p></li>\n</ul><h3 dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(54, 85, 179)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(54, 85, 179);color:inherit;\"> </mark></strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(54, 85, 179)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(54, 85, 179);color:inherit;\">4. Connect With Other Entrepreneurs</mark><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(54, 85, 179)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(54, 85, 179);color:inherit;\"> </mark></strong></span></h3><p dir=\"auto\">Beyond expert advisors, <strong>networking with other business owners</strong> is one of the best ways to gain real-world insights. Find <strong>free entrepreneur meetups</strong> to learn from those who have already started a business:</p><ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"earth_americas\" data-type=\"emoji\">🌎</span> <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"https://www.meetup.com/\"><strong>Meetup.com</strong></a> | Enter your ZIP code to find <strong>local business groups</strong> and networking events.</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"busts_in_silhouette\" data-type=\"emoji\">👥</span> <strong>Entrepreneur Groups</strong> | Look for free local meetups like startup workshops, small business owner networking, and industry-specific groups.</p></li>\n</ul><h3 dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><mark data-color=\"rgb(54, 85, 179)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(54, 85, 179);color:inherit;\"> Why This Step Matters </mark></span></h3><p dir=\"auto\">Before applying for funding, <strong>you </strong><span><strong>must understand the best options </strong>for your</span> business idea. Owners","body":"<p dir=\"auto\">Navigating business support programs can feel complicated—but you’re not alone. There are national offices and local agencies ready to help you <strong>get certified, find funding, and access free support</strong>.</p><p dir=\"auto\">This lesson will guide you through some of the most important <strong>federal and city-level resources</strong> you can use as a small business owner. Whether you’re looking for government contracts, certifications, or simply trying to locate your local support agency, this is a great place to start.</p><hr dir=\"auto\"><h3 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"compass\" data-type=\"emoji\">🧭</span> Federal Small Business Offices</h3><p dir=\"auto\">These government-run offices are designed to support <strong>small business participation in federal programs</strong>, including funding, contracts, and training.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span> <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://business.defense.gov/Work-with-us/Federal-Small-Business-Offices/\">Federal Small Business Offices</a>\n</h4><p dir=\"auto\">Explore how to work with the U.S. Department of Defense and connect with federal small business representatives in various agencies.</p><hr dir=\"auto\"><h3 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"pushpin\" data-type=\"emoji\">📌</span> National Small Business Portals</h3><p dir=\"auto\">These general portals can guide you to many different government services and small business tools:</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span> <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.usa.gov/small-business?source=busa\">USA.gov Small Business Portal</a>\n</h4><p dir=\"auto\">A great all-in-one resource for exploring small business programs, licenses, and government-run support.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span> <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://certifications.sba.gov/\">MySBA Certifications</a>\n</h4><p dir=\"auto\">Looking to become a <strong>certified small business</strong> for federal contracts or programs? This is the official SBA platform to get started with certifications.</p><hr dir=\"auto\"><h3 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"world_map\" data-type=\"emoji\">🗺</span> Local Government Tools: Find Help in Your Area</h3><p dir=\"auto\">Local programs are often easier to access and more tailored to your needs. These tools will help you find your <strong>county, city, and regional offices</strong>, where additional grants, licenses, or support may be offered.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span> <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://ce.naco.org/?find=true\">NACo County Explorer</a>\n</h4><p dir=\"auto\">Use this map-based tool to locate your <strong>county government</strong>, see what services they offer, and explore local small business programs.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span> <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.nlc.org/\">National League of Cities</a>\n</h4><p dir=\"auto\">Find out what your <strong>city government</strong> is doing to support small business owners. Search for city-specific programs, grants, and initiatives that could apply to you.</p><hr dir=\"auto\"><h3 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"mega\" data-type=\"emoji\">📣</span> Your Next Move</h3><p dir=\"auto\">Now that you have access to both <strong>federal-level</strong> and <strong>local-level</strong> tools, take a few minutes to:</p><ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Locate your city or county agency</strong> using the tools above.</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Bookmark the SBA Certification Portal</strong> if you plan to work with government grants or contracts.</p></li>\n</ul><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"check_mark\" data-type=\"emoji\">✔</span> In the next lesson, we’ll look at <strong>grant opportunities</strong> you can apply for now—from national programs to niche opportunities for underrepresented founders.</p><p dir=\"auto\">Let’s keep building!</p><p dir=\"auto\"><br dir=\"auto\"></p><p dir=\"auto\"> </p>","url":"https://lesko-help-2.mn.co/posts/business-nonprofits-career-federal-local-support-for-small-business-owners","postType":"coursework","publishedAt":null,"commentCount":0,"cheerCount":0,"creator":{"id":"Z2lkOi8vbWlnaHR5L1VzZXIvMTUxMjU4NDE","resourceId":"15125841","name":"Giulia May | Community Manager","avatarUrl":"https://media1-production-mightynetworks.imgix.net/asset/b227c7d1-697c-4519-b7e8-0b0f1c283b9b/58842506_880478525637716_1227268994818375680_n.jpeg?ixlib=rails-4.3.1&fm=jpg&q=100&auto=format&w=128&h=128&fit=crop&crop=faces&impolicy=Avatar"},"space":{"id":"Z2lkOi8vbWlnaHR5L0ZsZXhTcGFjZS8xODA4Mzk1OA","resourceId":"18083958","title":"Business - Nonprofits & Career"}}},{"cursor":"Mg","node":{"__typename":"Post","resourceId":"55581901","id":"Z2lkOi8vbWlnaHR5L1Bvc3QvNTU1ODE5MDE","title":"Links that were shared during ALL Business with Amber! It includes everything from choosing your business structure to opening a bank account and securing licenses.</p><blockquote dir=\"auto\" class=\"tiptap-blockquote-standard\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"bulb\" data-type=\"emoji\">💡</span> <strong>Important:</strong> In the United States, all business registrations and incorporations are handled on the <strong>state level</strong>. This means that <strong>fees, processes, and specific requirements vary depending on your state</strong>.<br dir=\"auto\">We highly recommend checking with your state’s <strong>Department of Commerce</strong> or <strong>Secretary of State</strong> to confirm details specific to your location.</p></blockquote><hr dir=\"auto\"><h3 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"check_mark_button\" data-type=\"emoji\">✅</span> Business Start-Up Checklist</h3><p dir=\"auto\">Each item builds on the one before. Read through each step carefully and keep track of your progress. Items marked with <strong>*</strong> need to be renewed annually. Items marked with <strong>*?</strong> may have unique renewal timelines—check locally.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">☐ 1. <strong>Select Your Business Name &amp; Legal Structure</strong>\n</h4><p dir=\"auto\">Decide on your business name and legal form: Sole Proprietorship, Partnership, LLC, or Corporation. This choice affects taxes, liability, and how you can raise funds.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">☐ 2. <strong>Research Your Business Name with the Secretary of State</strong>\n</h4><p dir=\"auto\">Research Business Name with your state’s Secretary of State’s website to make sure no other business has that name.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">☐ 3. <strong>Register the Legal Entity with Your State</strong> *</h4><p dir=\"auto\">File your business with the state government and obtain your <strong>Certificate of Incorporation</strong> or <strong>Articles of Organization</strong>.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">☐ 4. <strong>Get Your State Tax License and Set Up Tax Accounts</strong>\n</h4><p dir=\"auto\">Create a <strong>State Tax ID/account</strong> through your state’s tax division. This is different from your federal EIN and is essential for state-level tax compliance.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">☐ 5. <strong>Obtain Your Federal EIN from the IRS</strong>\n</h4><p dir=\"auto\">Apply online for your <strong>Employer Identification Number (EIN)</strong> from the IRS. This is necessary to open bank accounts, hire employees, and file taxes.<br dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"point_right\" data-type=\"emoji\">👉</span> Apply for an EIN here</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">☐ 6. <strong>Open a Business Bank or Credit Union Account</strong>\n</h4><p dir=\"auto\">Set up a <strong>business checking account</strong> at a trusted FDIC-insured bank or credit union using your EIN and state paperwork.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">☐ 7. <strong>Apply for a Local Business License</strong> *</h4><p dir=\"auto\">Depending on your location, you’ll need to register your business with your <strong>city or county office</strong> based on your physical address.</p><h4 dir=\"auto\">☐ 8. <strong>Get Professional Licenses or Permits (if required)</strong> *?</h4><p dir=\"auto\">If your business operates in a regulated industry (like health, finance, childcare, or food services), you may need specific licenses or inspections.<br dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"pushpin\" data-type=\"emoji\">📌</span> Renewal timelines vary. Always verify the terms for your specific license or permit.</p><p dir=\"auto\"><br dir=\"auto\"></p><h5 dir=\"auto\">Download your checklist here</h5><p dir=\"auto\"></p><a data-type=\"file-link\" dir=\"auto\">• <strong>Donation-based</strong> (people support you without expecting anything in return)<br dir=\"auto\">• <strong>Reward-based</strong> (supporters get early access, perks, or products)<br dir=\"auto\">• <strong>Equity-based</strong> (backers become investors in your company)<br dir=\"auto\">• <strong>Debt-based</strong> (you repay the funds like a loan)</p><p Coaching"}}},{"cursor":"Mg","node":{"__typename":"Post","resourceId":"83646154","id":"Z2lkOi8vbWlnaHR5L1Bvc3QvODM2NDYxNTQ","title":"Governement Contracting for Small Businesses","body":"<p dir=\"auto\">Did you know that the U.S. government is the <strong>largest buyer of goods and services</strong> in the world? Every year, billions of dollars in contracts are awarded to small businesses—just like yours. Whether you offer products, services, consulting, or manufacturing, <strong>government contracting</strong> can open a stable and lucrative revenue stream.</p><p dir=\"auto\">But breaking into federal, state, or local contracting requires understanding the process, registering correctly, and knowing where to look. This guide will walk you through the <strong>essential steps and tools</strong> to get started.</p><hr dir=\"auto\"><h3 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"one\" data-type=\"emoji\">1⃣</span> Register Your Business on <strong>SAM.gov</strong> (Your First Step)</h3><p dir=\"auto\">Before you can bid on federal contracts or subcontracts, you must <strong>register your business on SAM.gov</strong>. This is the official database where all contractors are listed.</p><ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span><strong> Entity Checklist</strong><br dir=\"auto\">Before registering, download the Entity Checklist (PDF) on SAM.gov to prepare all required documents.</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span><strong> Search Contracts</strong><br dir=\"auto\">Even before you're fully registered, browse available contracts to get familiar with opportunities.</p></li>\n</ul><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"point_right\" data-type=\"emoji\">👉</span> <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://sam.gov/\"><strong>Start here: SAM.gov Registration &amp; Search</strong></a></p><hr dir=\"auto\"><h3 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"two\" data-type=\"emoji\">2⃣</span> Know Your Codes: <strong>NAICS, PSC &amp; UNSPSC</strong>\n</h3><p dir=\"auto\">Government agencies use specific codes to classify businesses and their services/products. You’ll need these during registration and bidding.</p><ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li dir=\"auto\">\n<p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span><strong> NAICS Codes</strong><br dir=\"auto\">The North American Industry Classification System assigns a 6-digit number to describe your industry.</p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\"><li dir=\"auto\">\n<p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span>The following resources have both free and for-cost services. Please stick to the free resources:</p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span><a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"https://www.naics.com/\"><strong>https://www.naics.com/</strong></a></p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span><a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer noopener noreferrer noopener noreferrer\" href=\"https://naicscode.com/\"><strong>https://naicscode.com/</strong></a><strong> </strong></p></li>\n</ul>\n</li></ul>\n</li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span><strong> PSC Codes</strong><br dir=\"auto\">Product Service Codes (PSC) describe exactly what goods or services you're offering.</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span><strong> UNSPSC Codes</strong><br dir=\"auto\">Some agencies may also require UNSPSC Codes for additional classification.</p></li>\n</ul><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"bulb\" data-type=\"emoji\">💡</span> Make sure you’ve identified the correct codes before completing your SAM registration.</p><hr dir=\"auto\"><h3 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"three\" data-type=\"emoji\">3⃣</span> Get Support from <strong>APEX Accelerators</strong>\n</h3><p dir=\"auto\">You don’t have to navigate government contracting alone. <strong>APEX Accelerators</strong> (formerly PTACs) offer <strong>free counseling</strong> to help small businesses understand and succeed in federal contracting.</p><ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span><strong> Learn how to bid</strong></p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span><strong> Understand compliance rules</strong></p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span><strong> Get help with certifications</strong></p></li>\n</ul><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"point_right\" data-type=\"emoji\">👉</span> <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.apexaccelerators.us/\"><strong>Find a counselor near you: APEX Accelerators</strong></a></p><hr dir=\"auto\"><h3 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"four\" data-type=\"emoji\">4⃣</span> Use <strong>SBA Tools &amp; Programs</strong>\n</h3><p dir=\"auto\">The U.S. Small Business Administration (SBA) offers powerful tools to help you access set-aside contracts and navigate the system.</p><ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span><strong> Size Standards Tool</strong><br dir=\"auto\">Check if your business qualifies as a “small business” for federal contracting purposes.<br dir=\"auto\"><a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.sba.gov/federal-contracting/contracting-guide/size-standards/size-standards-tool\"><strong>Use the Tool</strong></a></p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span><strong> SBA Contracting Assistance Programs</strong><br dir=\"auto\">Explore programs like <strong>8(a)</strong>, <strong>HUBZone</strong>, and <strong>WOSB</strong> certifications to access exclusive contract opportunities.<br dir=\"auto\"><a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.sba.gov/federal-contracting/contracting-assistance-programs\"><strong>Learn More</strong></a></p></li>\n</ul><hr dir=\"auto\"><h3 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"five\" data-type=\"emoji\">5⃣</span> Payment Systems: <strong>PIEE</strong> &amp; More</h3><p dir=\"auto\">If you're contracting with the <strong>Department of Defense</strong> or its agencies, you’ll likely need to register on the <strong>Procurement Integrated Enterprise Environment (PIEE)</strong> to receive payments.</p><p dir=\"auto\">For other agencies, payment systems will vary—always confirm with the awarding agency which platform to use.</p><hr dir=\"auto\"><h3 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"six\" data-type=\"emoji\">6⃣</span> Learn the Rules: <strong>Federal Acquisition Regulation (FAR)</strong>\n</h3><p dir=\"auto\">The <strong>Federal Acquisition Regulation (FAR)</strong> outlines how federal contracts are issued and managed. It’s complex, so reviewing it with an APEX Accelerator counselor is highly recommended.</p><hr dir=\"auto\"><h3 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"seven\" data-type=\"emoji\">7⃣</span> Research &amp; Forecasting Tools</h3><p dir=\"auto\">Get ahead by knowing what agencies are buying and who they’re buying from:</p><ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span><strong> Agency Recurring Procurement Forecasts</strong><br dir=\"auto\">See what agencies plan to purchase in upcoming cycles.</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span><strong> USA Spending</strong><br dir=\"auto\">Track past contracts—who won them and for how much.</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"small_blue_diamond\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔹</span><strong> D-U-N-S Number</strong><br dir=\"auto\">While no longer required for SAM.gov, many other agencies still ask for a D-U-N-S number. It’s like a Social Security number for your business—good to have on hand.</p></li>\n</ul><hr dir=\"auto\"><h3 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"mega\" data-type=\"emoji\">📣</span> Your Next Move</h3><p dir=\"auto\"><br dir=\"auto\"></p><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"one\" data-type=\"emoji\">1⃣</span> Gather your documents using the <strong>SAM.gov Entity Checklist</strong>.</p><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"two\" data-type=\"emoji\">2⃣</span> Identify your <strong>NAICS</strong>, <strong>PSC</strong>, and (if needed) <strong>UNSPSC codes</strong>.</p><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"three\" data-type=\"emoji\">3⃣</span> Register on <strong>SAM.gov</strong> and book a free session with your local <strong>APEX Accelerator</strong> counselor.</p><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"four\" data-type=\"emoji\">4⃣</span> Explore <strong>SBA’s contracting assistance programs</strong> to see if you qualify for set-asides.</p><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"bulb\" data-type=\"emoji\">💡</span> Government contracting can feel overwhelming at first—but with the right steps and support, it becomes a <strong>powerful way to grow your business</strong>.</p><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"check_mark\" data-type=\"emoji\">✔</span> Once you're registered and familiar with the process, start bidding on smaller contracts to build experience—and watch new doors open!</p>","url":"https://lesko-help-2.mn.co/posts/business-nonprofits-career-governement-contracting-for-small-businesses","postType":"coursework","publishedAt":null,"commentCount":1,"cheerCount":0,"creator":{"id":"Z2lkOi8vbWlnaHR5L1VzZXIvMTUxMjU4NDE","resourceId":"15125841","name":"Giulia
