Benefits total about $400 Billion.</p><p dir=\"auto\">The total benefits available to ALL Americans is over $5 Trillion.<br dir=\"auto\">Veterans receive only about 8% of that total.</p><p dir=\"auto\">If you don’t find what you need in VA programs, there are hundreds of billions more in non-veteran programs you can apply for.</p><p dir=\"auto\">Don’t stop at “No.”</p><hr dir=\"auto\"><h1 dir=\"auto\"><strong>➽ 2 | One Office Doesn’t Know Everything</strong></h1><p dir=\"auto\">No single person or office knows ALL the programs available.</p><p dir=\"auto\">If someone tells you “You don’t qualify,” that only means you don’t qualify for THAT program.</p><p dir=\"auto\">There are hundreds more. Keep asking.</p><hr dir=\"auto\"><h1 dir=\"auto\"><strong>➽ 3 | First Stop: County Veterans Service Office (FREE!)</strong></h1><p dir=\"auto\">Make an appointment.</p><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"telephone_receiver\" data-type=\"emoji\">📞</span> 888-777-4443<br dir=\"auto\"><a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer nofollow\" href=\"https://nvf.org/veteran-service-officers/\">https://nvf.org/veteran-service-officers/</a></p><p dir=\"auto\">They get paid to help YOU.</p><hr dir=\"auto\"><h1 dir=\"auto\"><strong>➽ 4 | Search 400+ Additional Programs in Seconds</strong></h1><p dir=\"auto\">Go to <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer nofollow\" href=\"http://FindHelp.org\">FindHelp.org</a><br dir=\"auto\">Type your ZIP code<br dir=\"auto\">Search “Veterans”</p><p dir=\"auto\">Sample: Kensington, MD 20895<br dir=\"auto\"><a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer nofollow\" href=\"https://findhelp.org/search/text?term=veterans&amp;postal=20895\">https://findhelp.org/search/text?term=veterans&amp;postal=20895</a></p><p dir=\"auto\">If you don’t search, you don’t see.</p><hr dir=\"auto\"><h1 dir=\"auto\"><strong>➽ 5 | Benefits Can Cover Your Family Too</strong></h1><p dir=\"auto\">Many programs help spouses, children, caregivers, and surviving family members.</p><p dir=\"auto\">Don’t assume it’s just for you. dir=\"auto\">Now that you understand your financial situation and know what’s possible, it’s time to <strong>take action</strong> and start securing the help you need.</p><p dir=\"auto\">This step focuses on contacting <strong>trusted organizations</strong> that provide <strong>grants, financial aid, and bill assistance programs.</strong> These programs can help with <strong>rent, mortgage payments, utilities, medical bills, taxes, and even debt forgiveness.</strong></p><h3 dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><mark data-color=\"rgb(54, 85, 179)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(54, 85, 179);color:inherit;\"> 1. Watch Matthews's video first! </mark></span></h3><span class=\"mighty-video-wrapper\" contenteditable=\"false\" draggable=\"false\"><a class=\"mighty-video video-overlay nohover\" href=\"https://www.youtube.com/embed/BqCDBj6v-sw\" target=\"_blank\" type=\"video/youtube\" data-video-id=\"1739539974\" data-image-asset-id=\"119441263\"><img src=\"https://media1-production-mightynetworks.imgix.net/asset/180bf503-b6eb-4136-8bd6-cadb0ba6fbf8/Screenshot_2025-02-14_at_14.32.38.png?ixlib=rails-4.2.0&amp;fm=jpg&amp;q=75&amp;auto=format\" alt=\"Video\" class=\"fr-fil fr-dib attachment fr-draggable\" data-asset-id=\"119441263\"></a></span><p dir=\"auto\">These programs are part of the <strong>“giving economy”</strong>—a network of <strong>government offices and nonprofits</strong> offering financial assistance <strong>at no cost.</strong> Unlike for-profit services on Google that charge fees, these organizations are designed to <strong>help, not profit from your situation.</strong></p><h3 dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><mark data-color=\"rgb(54, 85, 179)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(54, 85, 179);color:inherit;\"> 2. Contact These 10 Key Organizations </mark></span></h3><ol dir=\"auto\">\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\"> FINDHELP.ORG</mark></strong></span><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\"> </mark> Visit <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.findhelp.org/\"><strong>FindHelp.org</strong></a> and enter your zip code to access <strong>thousands of local programs</strong> that provide <strong>grants and free financial support</strong> for rent, utilities, mortgage, food, and more.</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\"> STUDENT LOAN ASSISTANCE </mark></strong></span><strong> (StudentAid.gov) </strong>Visit <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://studentaid.gov/\"><strong>StudentAid.gov</strong></a> to explore <strong>loan forgiveness, repayment plans, and relief programs</strong> for student debt. Call their helpline to understand your options.</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\"> IRS OFFER IN COMPROMISE PROGRAM </mark></strong></span><strong> </strong>If you owe back taxes, the <strong>IRS can forgive up to 90% of your tax debt.</strong> Contact your <strong>local IRS office</strong> and ask about the <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"https://www.irs.gov/payments/offer-in-compromise\"><strong>Offer in Compromise program</strong></a> to see if you qualify.</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><strong> <mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\"> STATE TAX RELIEF PROGRAMS</mark></strong></span><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\"> </mark> </strong>Most states offer <strong>tax debt forgiveness programs.</strong> Contact your <strong>state comptroller or tax office</strong> to inquire about tax relief or hardship programs in your area. <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"https://taxadmin.org/tax-agencies/\"><strong>State Tax Agencies</strong></a></p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\"> MEDICAL BILL ASSISTANCE </mark></strong></span><strong> (Patient Advocate Foundation) </strong>Visit the <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.patientadvocate.org/\"><strong>Patient Advocate Foundation</strong></a> for help with <strong>medical bill assistance, co-pay grants, and financial aid</strong> for healthcare costs.</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\">PAN FOUNDATION</mark></strong> </span>– Visit <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"https://www.panfoundation.org/\"><strong>panfoundation.org</strong></a><strong> </strong> to access f<strong>inancial assistance programs</strong> that help individuals cover <strong>out-of-pocket medical expenses, including prescription costs, insurance premiums, and treatment-related expenses.</strong></p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\"> NEEDYMEDS.ORG </mark></strong></span><strong> </strong>Find <strong>free and low-cost prescription medication, medical services, and bill assistance</strong> at <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.needymeds.org/\"><strong>Needymeds.org</strong></a> or call <strong>1-800-503-6897</strong>.</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\"> HOSPITAL BILL FORGIVENESS </mark></strong></span><strong> (DollarFor.org) </strong>Many hospitals legally require forgiving<strong> medical bills</strong> based on income levels. Visit <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://dollarfor.org/\"><strong>DollarFor.org</strong></a> to see if you qualify for <strong>hospital bill relief</strong> and how to apply.</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\"> </mark></strong><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\">GOVERNMENT &amp; SOCIAL SECURITY DEPT ASSISTANCE </mark></strong></span><strong> </strong>If you owe <strong>unemployment insurance overpayments, Social Security repayments, or government loans,</strong> first <strong>contact the agency you owe</strong>—they often have hardship waivers or payment reductions. If they refuse to help, <strong>call your elected officials</strong> to advocate on your behalf.</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\"> BACK CHILD SUPPORT FORGIVENESS </mark></strong></span><strong> </strong>The <strong>Office of Child Support Services</strong> has <strong>state-by-state forgiveness programs</strong> that can <strong>reduce or eliminate child support debt.</strong> Check with your <strong>state child support office</strong> to see if you qualify.<br dir=\"auto\"><strong>Contact Your Elected Officials (CommonCause.org)</strong></p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\"><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" 160, 28);color:inherit;\">COMMON CAUSE</mark></strong></span><strong><mark data-color=\"rgb(44, 160, 28)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(44, 160, 28);color:inherit;\"> </mark></strong> If you’re struggling with <strong>government debts, fines, or legal financial obligations,</strong> your <strong>elected officials can intervene.</strong> Visit <a target=\"_new\" rel=\"noopener\" href=\"https://www.commoncause.org/\"><strong>CommonCause.org</strong></a> to find <strong>who represents you</strong> and ask for their help in resolving financial hardships.<br dir=\"auto\"><br dir=\"auto\"></p></li>\n</ol><h3 dir=\"auto\"><span style=\"color:rgb(255, 255, 255);\"><mark data-color=\"rgb(54, 85, 179)\" style=\"background-color:rgb(54, 85, 179);color:inherit;\"> TIP: How to Start With State Veteran Service Officers (Your First Stop)</mark></strong></h2><p dir=\"auto\">Before applying anywhere else, connect with your <strong>State Veteran Service Officer (VSO).</strong></p><h3 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"mag_right\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔎</span> VISIT: <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer nofollow\" href=\"http://nacvso.org\">nacvso.org</a>\n</h3><p dir=\"auto\">Here’s what to do:</p><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"round_pushpin\" data-type=\"emoji\">📍</span> Find your state<br dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"telephone_receiver\" data-type=\"emoji\">📞</span> Contact your local Veteran Service Officer<br dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"page_facing_up\" data-type=\"emoji\">📄</span> Ask for help identifying and applying for all benefits you qualify for</p><p dir=\"auto\">Research has shown that Veteran Service Officers are often able to help veterans secure <strong>more benefits than anyone else.</strong> This makes them the strongest starting point.</p><hr dir=\"auto\"><h2 dir=\"auto\"><strong><mark data-color=\"var(--tt-color-highlight-yellow)\" style=\"background-color:var(--tt-color-highlight-yellow);color:inherit;\">3. There are <strong>hundreds of thousands of additional programs</strong> that veterans and their families can qualify for.</p><h3 dir=\"auto\">\n<span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"mag_right\" data-type=\"emoji\">🔎</span> VISIT: <a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer nofollow\" href=\"http://findhelp.org\">findhelp.org</a>\n</h3><p dir=\"auto\">Steps:</p><p dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"round_pushpin\" data-type=\"emoji\">📍</span> Enter your zip code<br dir=\"auto\"><span dir=\"auto\" data-name=\"military_medal\" data-type=\"emoji\">🎖</span> Search “veterans”</p><p dir=\"auto\">In the example shared, one zip code produced <strong>414 organizations</strong> offering free programs, including:</p><ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\">Veteran training programs</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\">Aging and disability support for veterans</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\">Iraq veteran services</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\">Financial assistance</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\">Support programs</p></li>\n</ul><p dir=\"auto\">Every organization listed provides free assistance — they do not charge fees.</p><hr dir=\"auto\"><h2 dir=\"auto\"><strong><mark data-color=\"var(--tt-color-highlight-yellow)\" style=\"background-color:var(--tt-color-highlight-yellow);color:inherit;\">4. Use All Available Benefit Categories</mark></strong></h2><p dir=\"auto\">As a veteran, you may qualify for programs beyond traditional VA benefits, including:</p><ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\">Housing assistance</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\">Rent support</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\">Transportation help</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\">Medical services outside the VA system</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\">Training and education programs</p></li>\n</ul><p dir=\"auto\">If one office cannot help, move to another. There are many options.</p><hr dir=\"auto\"><h2 dir=\"auto\"><strong><mark data-color=\"var(--tt-color-highlight-yellow)\" style=\"background-color:var(--tt-color-highlight-yellow);color:inherit;\">5. Contact Your Elected Officials</mark></strong></h2><p dir=\"auto\">Your elected officials have staff whose job is to help constituents access benefits. They track how many people they assist and often help resolve benefit issues.</p><p dir=\"auto\">Do not overlook this resource.</p><hr dir=\"auto\"><h2 dir=\"auto\"><strong><mark data-color=\"var(--tt-color-highlight-yellow)\" style=\"background-color:var(--tt-color-highlight-yellow);color:inherit;\">6. Avoid Paid Services</mark></strong></h2><p dir=\"auto\">Be cautious of websites ending in <strong>.com</strong> that offer to help you get benefits — many charge fees or attempt to take a portion of your benefits.</p><p dir=\"auto\">Instead:</p><ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\">Use <strong>.org</strong> and <strong>.gov</strong> resources</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\">Contact official Veteran Service Officers</p></li>\n<li dir=\"auto\"><p dir=\"auto\">Speak with organizations that are funded to help you — not businesses trying to sell services</p></li>\n</ul><p dir=\"auto\">Start with free, official resources.
